This paper addresses the challenge of predicting water potability through machine learning and deep learning algorithms. It introduces a novel feature augmentation algorithm, AquaAugmentor, to enhance the predictive performance of these models for low-dimensional datasets. Utilizing a dataset that includes chemical attributes of water, such as pH, hardness, solids, chloramines, sulfate, and others. This study evaluates the performance of the models with and without AquaAugmentor. Each model applied to classify water as potable or non-potable and its performance is then evaluated and compared based on test accuracy and AUC score. The results highlight the strengths and limitations of our proposed algorithm, providing insights into the most effective techniques for improving the predictive performance of water quality classification. This study contributes to the broader efforts of ensuring safe water access and serves as a framework for employing machine learning in environmental quality assessments. The findings aim to assist researchers, policymakers, and public health officials in making informed decisions based on reliable machine learning predictions.

</details>


### [110] [Knowledge-Assisted Multi-Graph Dependency Learning for Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection in Multi-Stage Industrial Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15799)
*Jaeyeong Lee,Taeseong Yoon,Wonmo Koo,Heeyoung Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种知识辅助的多图框架，用于多阶段工业过程中的多元时间序列异常检测。该方法构建了三种互补图（纯数据驱动图、两种融合过程知识约束的图），并采用多图注意力网络进行建模。在真实工业数据集上的实验表明，融合过程知识显著提升了异常检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GNN的多元时间序列异常检测方法通常忽略了关键的过程知识，即使考虑了这些知识，也难以无缝地将其融入现有模型，导致性能次优。

Method: 提出知识辅助的多图框架：(1) 构建三种互补图——一个纯数据驱动图和两个通过过程知识结构约束精炼的图；(2) 采用多图注意力网络融合这些图，实现更准确鲁棒的依赖关系建模。

Result: 在两个真实多阶段工业数据集上的综合实验表明，融合过程知识显著增强了异常检测性能。

Conclusion: 显式地将过程知识融入图学习能够增强传感器依赖关系建模，有效提升多阶段工业过程中多元时间序列异常检测的性能。

Abstract: Industrial processes often generate complex, interdependent time-series data from multiple sensors across multiple stages, forming complex dependencies among variables and process stages. Effective monitoring and timely anomaly detection of these time series through multivariate time series anomaly detection (MTAD) is crucial for preventing failures and ensuring the reliability of automated systems. Graph neural networks (GNNs) have advanced MTAD by leveraging data-driven graphs to model complex dependencies among variables, effectively capturing relational structures within multivariate time series to enhance anomaly detection performance. However, existing GNN-based approaches often overlook critical process knowledge, and even when this knowledge is considered, seamlessly incorporating it into existing models remains inherently challenging, leading to suboptimal performance. To address this limitation, we propose a knowledge-assisted multi-graph framework for modeling sensor dependencies in multi-stage industrial processes for MTAD, which explicitly incorporates process knowledge into graph learning to enhance dependency modeling and improve anomaly detection performance. Our method constructs three complementary graphs: one purely data-driven and two refined by integrating structural constraints derived from process knowledge. To effectively leverage these graphs for anomaly detection, we employ a multi-graph attention network, enabling a more accurate and robust representation of complex dependencies. Comprehensive experiments on two real-world, multi-stage industrial datasets demonstrate that incorporating process knowledge substantially enhances anomaly detection performance.

</details>


### [111] [QUADS: Stabilizing NVFP4 Reinforcement Learning for MoE via QUantization-error Alignment across Dual Sides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15810)
*Zhengyang Zhuge,Hao Yu,Xin Wang,Zheng Li,Yizhong Cao,Dayiheng Liu,Jianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对MoE大语言模型强化学习中的NVFP4低精度推理不稳定问题，提出QUADS方法，通过训练端非对称量化感知训练和推理端残差激活补偿，在保持W4A4 GEMM效率的同时实现BF16级精度，吞吐量比FP8提升约16%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE RL的rollout生成是主要瓶颈，需要低精度加速（如FP8）。NVFP4格式理论上提供更高吞吐，但直接应用到MoE RL中会导致训练崩溃（约150步后collapse），需要解决这一稳定性问题。

Method: 提出QUADS（Quantization-error Alignment Across Dual Sides）：1）训练端：非对称量化感知训练（Asymmetric QAT），仅对权重进行fake-quantization，激活保持未量化以对齐；2）推理端：残差激活补偿（Residual Activation Compensation），修正高误差激活通道，同时保留原生W4A4 GEMM。

Result: 在多个MoE RL基准上，QUADS达到BF16级精度，相比朴素NVFP4 RL，pass@1平均提升21.49个百分点，rollout吞吐量比FP8高出约16%。

Conclusion: 通过识别激活误差（而非权重误差）是FP4 RL不稳定的主导因素，QUADS双端对齐量化误差的方法有效稳定了NVFP4在MoE RL中的训练，实现了精度与效率的兼顾。

Abstract: Rollout generation is a major bottleneck in Reinforcement Learning (RL) for Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) Large Language Models, motivating low-precision rollout acceleration such as FP8. As an emerging low-precision format, NVFP4 combines fine-grained scaling for accuracy preservation with native W4A4 FP4 GEMMs for higher throughput than FP8. However, we find that directly applying NVFP4 to MoE RL rollout is impractical. NVFP4 rollout with BF16 training collapses after roughly 150 steps, accompanied by rapidly growing rollout-trainer log-probability gaps. Through training-inference error analysis and controlled ablations, we identify activation error, rather than weight error, as the dominant source of FP4 RL instability: weights can be synchronized and aligned by a shared quantization-dequantization path, whereas activations are recomputed online and error is amplified by the coarse E2M1 grid. Therefore, to stabilize NVFP4 RL for MoE, we propose QUantization-error Alignment across Dual Sides (QUADS). On the trainer side, we introduce Asymmetric Quantization-Aware Training fake-quantizing weights while keeping activations unquantized for better alignment. On the rollout side, Residual Activation Compensation corrects high-error activation channels while preserving native W4A4 GEMMs. In our MoE RL experiments on several benchmarks, QUADS achieves BF16-level accuracy, improves average pass@1 by 21.49 points over naive NVFP4 RL, and delivers ~16% higher rollout throughput than FP8.

</details>


### [112] [Graph Coloring Approach to Solving Sudoku with Oscillatory Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15814)
*Filip Sabo,Aida Todri-Sanial*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于振荡神经网络（ONN）的数独求解器，通过将数独问题转化为图着色问题，并改进现有图着色求解器使其计算更高效、引入额外约束项，在4×4和9×9数独上显著优于已有HNN和ONN求解器，实现了近乎完美的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用振荡神经网络（ONN）这一基于物理的计算范式，求解带约束的组合优化问题（数独），以克服传统HNN和ONN求解器准确率不足的问题。

Method: 将数独问题形式化为图着色问题；修改已有图着色ONN求解器，降低计算开销；引入额外的约束项确保数独规则（行、列、宫格内数字不重复）被满足。

Result: 在4×4数独上达到近乎完美的准确率；在9×9数独上针对不同未知数字数量也取得了相当高的准确率；显著优于现有HNN和ONN求解器。

Conclusion: 所提出的ONN求解器能有效解决数独这类约束组合优化问题，在准确率上有显著提升，展示了ONN作为物理计算范式的潜力。

Abstract: Oscillatory Neural Networks (ONNs) present an attractive physics-based computing paradigm rooted in the dynamics of a network of typically fully coupled oscillators aiming to minimize an underlying energy function. In this paper, we propose an ONN-based solver for one well-known constrained combinatorial optimization problem, namely a Sudoku, by formulating the problem as a Graph Coloring problem. By modifying the already existing Graph Coloring solver to a computationally cheaper version and introducing an additional term ensuring the fulfillment of the Sudoku constraints, our solver is shown to significantly outperform the existing HNN- and ONN solvers in terms of accuracy. In particular, we are able to achieve nearly flawless accuracies on $4 \times 4$ as well as rather high accuracies on $9 \times 9$ Sudoku puzzles for different numbers of unknown digits.

</details>


### [113] [In-context learning of closed form solution to simple linear regression task using transformer with linear self-attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15819)
*Katsuyuki Hagiwara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个线性自注意力Transformer，通过层归一化近似实现最小二乘估计的闭式解（而非梯度下降），并在L1正则化训练中验证了该实现的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明Transformer能通过梯度下降实现回归问题的上下文学习，但尚不清楚这些实现是否真正通过训练获得。本文旨在探索是否可以通过闭式解而非近似梯度下降来实现上下文学习。

Method: 构建具有线性自注意力的Transformer，利用层归一化（Layer Normalization）来近似最小二乘估计的闭式解析解，替代传统的梯度下降近似方法。

Result: 实验表明，在目标输出为最小二乘估计且采用L1正则化训练的Transformer中，该闭式解实现主要被模型采用。

Conclusion: Transformer可以通过层归一化实现最小二乘估计的闭式解，而非必须依赖梯度下降算法，这为理解Transformer的上下文学习机制提供了新的视角。

Abstract: In-context learning is a remarkable property of transformers and has recently received a lot of interest. In many studies of in-context learning, it has been shown that transformers are capable of implementing solver for linear and non-linear regression problems, in which the most of them implement gradient descent algorithm. However, it is still unclear whether those implementations have actually been acquired through training. In this paper, we construct a transformer with linear self-attention, which in-context learns the least squares estimate in a simple regression task. The point here is that the closed form (analytical) solution is approximately obtained by using layer normalization rather than an approximate solution based on gradient descent algorithm. Then, we show an experimental example, in which our implementation is mainly used in the transformer trained with l1 regularization when the target output is the least squares estimate.

</details>


### [114] [Data-Native Global Optimization for Big Data K-means Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15835)
*Ravil Mussabayev,Rustam Mussabayev,Zukhra Yerdaliyeva,Kuldeyev Nursultan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Big-means++算法，通过在多随机样本诱导的替代景观上执行局部K-means优化，实现对任意高维大数据的可扩展全局聚类，并采用流动优势策略、几何尺度抖动机制、多智能体异步搜索和自动收敛检测等技术，在22个数据集上超越11种对比算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大数据的MSSC聚类问题是NP难的，现有方法要么陷入局部最优，要么需要高昂的元启发式混合方案。目标是在固定特征空间中处理任意多（甚至无限）观测值，而算法只能访问有限随机样本。

Method: Big-means++通过以下机制实现全局搜索：(1) 在多个随机样本诱导的经验MSSC代理景观上执行局部K-means优化，利用样本间变异性实现全局搜索；(2) 流动优势策略（flowing-incumbent）跨样本传递质心状态，无回滚操作；(3) 几何缩放样本大小的抖动机制（shaking），探索多分辨率代理景观；(4) 多智能体异步竞争系统，汇聚多样化随机轨迹的集体搜索智能；(5) 自动收敛检测机制，在达到高质量解后及时终止搜索。

Result: 在22个数据集上与11种对比算法进行实验，验证了Big-means++的有效性、效率和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Big-means++通过精心设计样本代理景观的遍历策略，将局部K-means优化组织为数据原生的全局搜索过程，实现了大数据的可扩展全局聚类，且提供了通用的速度-质量平衡控制。

Abstract: Big data clustering remains challenging: the Minimum Sum-of-Squares Clustering (MSSC) problem underlying K-means is NP-hard, and existing methods either reach poor local minima or require prohibitive metaheuristic hybrids. We target arbitrarily tall data: a fixed feature space may contain arbitrarily many, possibly infinitely many, observations, while the algorithm accesses only finite random samples. We propose Big-means++, an algorithm achieving scalability and global-search quality by curating inputs to MSSC optimization on big data. It orchestrates local K-means refinements into a data-native global search for big data clustering. Rather than optimizing the full-data MSSC objective, Big-means++ traverses sample-induced surrogate landscapes. Each sample defines a distinct empirical MSSC approximation with a perturbed local-optimum structure, turning sample-to-sample variation into a global-search mechanism. Unlike Big-means, a flowing-incumbent strategy propagates centroid state across empirical landscapes through K-means refinements on fresh samples without rollback to a best-so-far solution. This increases mobility and favors stable, high-quality configurations across approximations of the full-data structure. A new shaking mechanism varies sample size geometrically, broadening the surrogate landscapes explored across resolution scales, accounting for cluster imbalance, and improving solution quality. A competitive multi-agent system asynchronously explores independent sampled landscapes, transforming diverse stochastic trajectories into collective search intelligence. Automatic convergence detection stops each agent after attaining a high-quality solution but before further search risks degrading it, while providing a universal speed-quality control. Experiments on 22 datasets against 11 competing algorithms demonstrate the effectiveness, efficiency, and robustness of Big-means++.

</details>


### [115] [ContinuityBench: A Benchmark and Systems Study of Stateful Failover in Multi-Provider LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15899)
*Vishal Pandey,Gopal Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大语言模型生产部署中，主要供应商故障导致的无状态故障转移机制会丢失对话历史的问题，本文提出两种新指标（CPR和CLO）、一种有状态多供应商代理架构（历史转发策略）以及一个开源评估工具 continuity-bench。实验表明，该有状态代理在750次故障转移事件中实现了99.20%的连续性保留率，而标准无状态架构接近0%。同时提出带抖动的异步指数退避策略以防止级联重试风暴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高性能API可用性保障无法确保对话连续性，无状态故障转移虽维持了正常运行时间，却会静默丢弃对话历史，严重破坏用户体验。目前缺乏对会话连续性保留的量化指标和解决方案。

Method: 提出连续性保留率（CPR）和连续性延迟开销（CLO）两个量化指标；设计基于历史转发策略的有状态多供应商代理架构；开发开源评估工具continuity-bench用于高压下上下文保留的压力测试。

Result: 在750次故障转移事件的实证评估中，有状态代理达到99.20% CPR（95%置信区间：98.27%-99.63%），成功将深度对话上下文迁移至备用供应商；标准无状态架构的保留率接近0%。

Conclusion: 该研究为构建鲁棒的、保留状态的多模型推理系统提供了原则性基础，其中带抖动的异步指数退避策略对于防止严格限流后备API的级联重试风暴至关重要。

Abstract: In production large language model (LLM) deployments, high API availability guarantees do not equate to conversational continuity. When a primary provider experiences an outage or strict rate-limiting, naive stateless failover mechanisms successfully maintain uptime but silently discard conversation history, severely disrupting the user experience. To rigorously quantify and resolve this failure mode, we introduce two novel metrics: Continuity Preservation Rate (CPR) and Continuity Latency Overhead (CLO). We propose a stateful, multi-provider proxy architecture utilizing a History-Forwarding strategy to seamlessly reconstruct conversational state across heterogeneous LLM endpoints during failover events. Furthermore, we release continuity-bench, https://github.com/Vishal-sys-code/continuity-bench, an open evaluation harness designed to stress-test context preservation under high-concurrency provider failure conditions. Our empirical evaluation ($N=750$ failover events) demonstrates that our stateful proxy achieves a 99.20\% CPR [95\% CI: 98.27\%, 99.63\%], cleanly transferring deep conversational context to fallback providers, compared to a near-0\% preservation rate for standard stateless architectures. Finally, we characterize failover latency distributions, identifying the critical necessity of asynchronous exponential backoff with jitter to prevent cascading retry storms against strict-limit fallback APIs. Our results provide a principled foundation for building robust, state-preserving multi-model inference systems.

</details>


### [116] [A Semiparametric Framework for Stochastic Fundamental Diagram Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15907)
*Pengnan Chi,Xiaoliang Ma,Magnus Jansson,Magnus Nordenvaad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种半参数随机基本图（SFD）建模框架，通过设计满足物理约束的函数形式并结合神经网络结构，实现对交通密度-流量关系的概率建模，在真实数据集上优于现有基线方法，尤其在拥堵状态下表现出更优的概率准确性与不确定性量化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机交通流模型难以同时满足严格的物理约束（如密度-流量关系的单调性、有界性）和捕捉复杂非线性模式的灵活性，亟需一种既能保证理论合理性又能适应数据复杂性的建模方法。

Method: 提出半参数SFD框架：①设计满足物理约束的特制函数形式；②引入神经网络结构以捕获非线性模式；③推导矩匹配方程组，将物理约束转化为条件分布参数化，并证明位置-尺度分布族存在唯一解；④扩展至非位置-尺度分布（含边界约束情形）。

Result: 在真实交通数据集上，所提方法在概率预测准确性和不确定性量化方面一致优于代表性基线模型，尤其在拥堵（高密度）区域表现突出。

Conclusion: 该框架为随机交通流建模提供了理论严谨且灵活的基础，有效平衡了物理约束与数据驱动灵活性，具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: The stochastic fundamental diagram (SFD) provides a probabilistic description of the relationship between traffic density and flow or speed, enabling uncertainty-aware traffic modeling. However, existing stochastic models frequently struggle to accommodate rigorous physical constraints while retaining sufficient flexibility to capture complex nonlinear patterns. To address this, we propose a novel semiparametric SFD modeling framework by leveraging specially designed functional forms. These functions intrinsically satisfy physical constraints defined on the moments of the conditional flow distribution given traffic density while incorporating neural-network-based structures to capture complex empirical patterns. We derive a system of moment-matching equations to convert physical constraints into the parameterization of the conditional distribution, proving that a unique solution exists for the location-scale family of distributions, thereby guaranteeing model well-posedness. Furthermore, we demonstrate that the framework can be extended to non-location-scale distributions, including those requiring additional boundary constraints. Empirical evaluations on a real-world dataset reveal that our approach consistently outperforms representative baselines, delivering superior probabilistic accuracy and robust uncertainty quantification, particularly in congested regimes. Overall, the proposed framework provides a theoretically grounded and flexible foundation for stochastic traffic flow modeling.

</details>


### [117] [(MPO)$^2$: Multivariate Polynomial Optimization based on Matrix Product Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15916)
*Niccolò Ciolli,Anders Vestergaard Nørskov,Michael Kastoryano,Petr Taborsky,Morten Mørup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出(MPO)²框架，结合矩阵乘积算子(MPO)特征嵌入与紧凑多项式权重张量，实现阶数无关的多项式函数逼近，在回归与分类任务上优于现有张量分解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多项式模型的系数张量随阶数指数增长，现有张量化方法（CP分解秩受限、TT分解特征顺序依赖）存在局限性，亟需一种特征顺序无关且表达力强的多项式逼近框架。

Method: 提出(MPO)²框架，将学习的MPO特征嵌入与紧凑多项式权重张量结合，支持投影、卷积、掩码等结构化算子以施加权重张量对称性，实现特征顺序无关的多项式表示。

Result: 在回归与分类基准测试中，(MPO)²优于现有基于张量分解的多项式模型，证明了其在高效多项式函数逼近中的灵活性和有效性。

Conclusion: (MPO)²是一种灵活、高效且特征顺序无关的多项式函数逼近新框架，为张量化多项式模型提供了更好的替代方案。

Abstract: Central to machine learning and signal processing is the ability to perform universal function approximation and learn complex input-output relationships from limited numbers of observations. Multivariate polynomial models offer a natural way to express such relationships through multiplicative feature interactions, but their coefficient tensors grow exponentially in size with the polynomial degree. Existing tensorized polynomial models reduce this cost, yet canonical polyadic decompositions have rank-limited expressivity, and tensor train formulations are feature order dependent. We introduce Multivariate Polynomial Optimization based on Matrix Product Operators (MPO)$^2$, a framework that combines learned MPO feature embeddings with compact polynomial weight tensors. This yields feature order independent polynomial representations that can incorporate structured operators such as projections, convolutions, and masks for weight tensor symmetries. Across regression and classification benchmarks, (MPO)$^2$ improves over existing tensor decomposition based polynomial models and provides a flexible alternative for efficient polynomial function approximation.

</details>


### [118] [On the Failure of Boundary-Seeking Distillation in Bottlenecked Generative Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15919)
*Mohamed Amine Kina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了无数据知识蒸馏在自编码器架构中的适用性，发现边界搜索原则（如CAKE方法）在瓶颈式生成架构中存在根本性缺陷，并提出流形感知合成作为有效替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究针对分类器设计的边界搜索蒸馏原则（如CAKE）是否能直接扩展到自编码器的无数据蒸馏场景。

Method: 将连续重构任务重新表述为密集的逐特征分类任务，使解码器输出分类logits；在MNIST数据集上进行实验对比；分析CAKE在瓶颈式生成架构中的梯度冲突问题；提出流形感知合成方法。

Result: CAKE的边界搜索目标在瓶颈式生成架构中产生严重的梯度冲突，无法生成有效的边界样本；流形感知合成能完全避免这些冲突，成为有效的基线方法。

Conclusion: 边界搜索原则不适用于自编码器蒸馏，流形感知合成是更优的无数据生成式蒸馏方案。

Abstract: Data-free knowledge distillation transfers the knowledge encoded in a teacher model to a student model without access to the original training data. Prior work such as Contrastive Abductive Knowledge Extraction (CAKE) achieves this for classifiers by synthesizing samples near the teacher's decision boundary. In this work, we investigate whether this boundary-seeking principle extends to autoencoder distillation through experiments on the MNIST dataset . To enable a direct comparison, we reformulate continuous reconstruction as a dense, per-feature classification task, allowing the decoder to output categorical logits. We show that boundary-seeking objectives are fundamentally ill-posed in bottlenecked generative architectures. CAKE operates on a single, instance-level objective, but a decoder acts as an array of tightly coupled, feature-level classifiers constrained by a shared low-dimensional bottleneck. Independently sampling contrastive targets for these coupled outputs violates the geometry of the learned latent manifold and produces severe gradient conflicts instead of informative boundary samples. Manifold-aware synthesis bypasses these conflicts entirely and establishes an effective baseline for data-free generative distillation.

</details>


### [119] [Knowledge-Guided Cross-Modal Fusion for Adult-to-Pediatric ECG Transfer via Label-Conditioned Contrastive Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15928)
*Xinran Liu,Yuwen Li,Hongxiang Gao,Heyang Xu,Jianqing Li,Zongmin Wang,Chengyu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PEACE是一个知识引导的多模态ECG-文本对齐框架，通过诊断标签条件化对齐（而非全局文本融合）实现成人到儿科ECG的跨域迁移，在零样本、少样本和全微调设置下均显著提升了儿科ECG诊断的AUC性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主要在成人ECG上预训练的模型迁移到儿科ECG时效果差，因为儿科标签稀缺；且现有多模态ECG-文本方法仅在全局样本级别对齐，混淆了共现诊断的证据，限制了跨域迁移能力。

Method: PEACE包含三个核心组件：(1) 将每个诊断按节律、形态、ST-T轴描述，为每份记录构建正标签的描述token（三个轴token+融合嵌入）；(2) 标签查询网络（LQN）以诊断标签为查询，跨注意力作用于ECG token和轴token；(3) 标签集感知双向对比学习（LSBC）对齐共享诊断的ECG特征与融合嵌入；(4) 课程自适应融合（CAF）根据分类损失和平滑的训练进度动态调节对齐强度。知识分支仅用于训练监督，推理仅需ECG信号。

Result: 在ZZU-pECG上，PEACE在零样本、50样本和全微调下达到59.39%、81.74%和91.56%的宏平均AUC；在PTB-XL上微调后达到96.90%的宏平均AUC；零样本显著优于域适应基线，50样本与DANN相当；消融实验表明标签条件化的知识对齐（而非全局文本融合）是儿科迁移增益的关键。

Conclusion: 标签条件化的知识对齐策略能有效弥合成人与儿科ECG之间的域差距，在标签稀缺场景下显著优于全局文本对齐和域适应方法，为跨年龄段ECG诊断提供了一种实用的预训练-微调框架。

Abstract: Adult and pediatric electrocardiogram (ECG) interpretation relies on age-sensitive criteria, and models pretrained mainly on adult ECGs often transfer poorly to pediatric populations when pediatric labels are scarce. Existing multimodal ECG--text methods typically align waveforms and text at the global sample level, entangling evidence from co-occurring diagnoses and limiting transfer under this gap. We propose Pediatric-Adult ECG Alignment via Cross-modal Enhancement (PEACE), a knowledge-guided framework pretrained on the largely adult MIMIC-IV ECG corpus. PEACE describes each diagnosis along rhythm, morphology, and ST--T axes and, per recording, composes only positive-label descriptors into three axis tokens and a fused embedding. A label query network (LQN) uses diagnostic labels as queries to cross-attend over ECG tokens and axis tokens, while label set aware bidirectional contrastive learning (LSBC) aligns pooled ECG features with the fused embedding when recordings share diagnoses. Curriculum adaptive fusion (CAF) gates alignment strength according to smoothed classification loss and training progress, limiting disruption during early optimization. The knowledge branch is used only for training supervision; inference uses ECG signals alone. On ZZU-pECG, PEACE reaches macro average AUCs of 59.39%, 81.74%, and 91.56% under zero-shot, 50-shot, and full fine-tuning, with the clearest gains over foundation and knowledge-pretraining baselines under limited supervision; versus domain adaptation initializations, zero-shot improves substantially while 50-shot AUC is comparable to DANN. After fine-tuning on PTB-XL, PEACE reaches 96.90% macro average AUC over nine harmonized labels. Ablations confirm that label-conditioned knowledge alignment, rather than global text fusion, is the key driver of pediatric transfer gains.

</details>


### [120] [An Exploratory Study of Single Channel Surface Electromyography for Hand Gesture Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15972)
*Daanish Hindustani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探索了使用单通道表面肌电信号（sEMG）结合轻量级机器学习架构（NN、KNN、SVM）对十种手势进行分类的可行性。通过提取时域、频域、高阶过零和相对强度特征，结合Pearson相关性过滤和降维技术，在单通道条件下使用紧凑型神经网络实现了高达90%的分类准确率，为低成本、低功耗手势识别应用提供了可行方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多通道sEMG传感器阵列和计算密集型模型在实际低功耗和嵌入式系统中部署受限，因此需要探索使用更少的传感器通道和更轻量级的机器学习模型来实现准确的手势识别。

Method: 将原始sEMG信号转化为综合特征表示（时域、频域、高阶过零、相对强度特征），使用Pearson相关性过滤去除高度相关特征以减少冗余，选择性应用LDA和PCA进行降维，系统评估了前馈神经网络（NN）、K近邻（KNN）和支持向量机（SVM）三种分类器在四项实验中的表现。

Result: 结合时域和频域特征、Pearson过滤以及紧凑型神经网络，在有限的时空信息条件下实现了高达90%的分类准确率。

Conclusion: 单通道sEMG系统在低成本、低功耗手势识别应用中具有巨大潜力，能够在资源受限条件下提供有效的识别性能。

Abstract: Accurate hand gesture recognition using surface electromyography (sEMG) typically relies on multichannel sensor arrays and computationally intensive models. This limits practical deployment in low-power and embedded systems. This study investigates the feasibility of classifying ten hand gestures using a single sEMG channel combined with lightweight machine learning architectures. Raw sEMG signals were transformed into a comprehensive feature-based representation, including time-domain, frequency-domain, higher-order-crossing, and relative-intensity features. Feature redundancy was reduced using Pearson correlation filtering and the removal of highly correlated features, while dimensionality-reduction techniques (LDA and PCA) were applied selectively. Three classifiers, a feed-forward neural network (NN), k-nearest neighbors (KNN), and a support vector machine (SVM), were systematically evaluated across four experiments. Results demonstrate that combining time and frequency features with Pearson filtering and a compact NN can achieve up to 90 percent accuracy, even with limited temporal and spatial information. These findings highlight the potential of single-channel sEMG systems for cost-effective, low-power gesture-recognition applications.

</details>


### [121] [DebrisTracer: Reliable Tracking in Hypervelocity Impact Fast Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.15986)
*Théophane Loloum,Fabien Vivodtzev,David Hébert,Baptiste Reynier,Michel Arrigoni,Julien Tierny*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DebrisTracer框架，基于拓扑追踪和领域知识扩展，实现超高速撞击碎片图像的可靠自动追踪与物理验证，在质量预测、深度预测和碎片分群方面优于现有工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超高速撞击实验产生大量碎片，可靠估计碎片质量和速度分布对航空航天应用至关重要，但现有工具在噪声高、数据集特异的成像环境中追踪精度不足。

Method: 在现成的基于关键点提取与匹配的拓扑追踪框架基础上，融入领域知识和物理假设进行扩展，实现碎片的自动追踪与可解释的可视化分析。

Result: 在多种撞击角度和物理条件下，该方法在预测撞击质量分布和弹坑深度轮廓方面显著优于领域专家使用的现有工具；统计摘要能够可视化识别碎片群体中的不同物理状态，验证并细化专家先验预期。

Conclusion: DebrisTracer能准确可靠地追踪超高速撞击碎片，提供可解释的时空分析，有助于航空航天领域对碎片分布和撞击物理的深入理解。

Abstract: This application paper presents DebrisTracer, a framework for the reliable tracking of debris in hypervelocity impact fast imaging. These noisy and highly specific datasets capture the ejection of a large number of debris fragments after the impact of a projectile launched at hypervelocity into a target material. The reliable estimation of debris mass and speed distributions is of major importance in aerospace applications. We document how to extend an off-the-shelf topology tracking framework based on critical point extraction and matching, in order to incorporate domain knowledge and physical assumptions. Our approach automatically produces an accurate and reliable debris tracking, enabling an interpretable visual analysis of this complex space-time phenomenon. Extensive experiments demonstrate the accuracy improvements provided by our approach over established tools used by domain experts in terms of physical validation, specifically via the prediction of the experimental ejected mass and crater depth profiles. We illustrate the utility of our approach across several use cases (with varying impact angles and physics). We show that our statistical summaries enable the visual identification of distinct regimes within the debris population, corroborating and refining prior expectations of domain experts. Our database and our C++ implementation are available at this address: https://github.com/tloloum/DebrisTracer.

</details>


### [122] [Revisiting data-driven dynamic security assessment with a tabular foundation model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16031)
*Olayiwola Arowolo,Maosheng Yang,Jochen Cremer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用表格基础模型（TFM）通过上下文学习实现数据驱动的电力系统预故障动态安全评估，无需为每个预想事故单独训练模型，仅需极少量标注样本即可达到高精度，并能推广到未见过的预想事故。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动动态安全评估方法需要大量标注数据库，且为每个预想事故单独训练一个模型，导致维护成本高；同时训练好的模型难以泛化到未见过的预想事故。本文旨在解决这两个局限性。

Method: 采用表格基础模型（TFM），通过上下文学习实现稳定性评估，无需重新训练或超参数优化；引入电气距离坐标（EDC）作为连续特征以增强泛化能力；单个TFM可同时评估多个预想事故。

Result: 在IEEE 68节点系统上，单个TFM仅需每个预想事故120个标注样本即可达到约90%的平均Macro F1分数，比传统方法少两个数量级；对于未见过的预想事故，仅需10个标注样本结合EDC编码即可匹配最优迁移学习模型的性能。

Conclusion: 该初步研究为开发和部署用于电力系统运行的基座模型铺平了道路，可应用于多种运行任务。

Abstract: Data-driven pre-fault dynamic security assessment (DSA) rapidly evaluates the dynamic risk of credible contingencies on a power system using machine learning. Existing approaches face two limitations. First, they require a large labelled database for training, with a separate model trained, tuned, and maintained for each contingency in a potentially long list of credible contingencies. Second, the trained models generalize poorly to unseen contingencies. This work addresses the limitations by using a tabular foundation model (TFM) that assesses stability through in-context learning, requiring no retraining or hyperparameter optimization. A single TFM can assess many contingencies at once, removing the need for one model per classifier. We also characterize when the use of electrical distance coordinates (EDC) as continuous features enables generalization of TFM to unseen contingencies and when they do not, demonstrating how a few labelled samples can reliably improve generalization. Through comprehensive case studies on the IEEE 68-bus system, we show that a single TFM attains an average Macro F1 score of about 90% with only 120 labelled samples per contingency, roughly two orders of magnitude fewer than conventionally assumed, without any model retraining or hyperparameter tuning. For new/unseen contingencies, we show that using just 10 labelled samples of the new contingency with EDC encoding matches the best achievable transfer learning oracle model, which requires fully labelled data and is not deployable in practice. Overall, this initial study paves the way towards developing and deploying foundation models for power system operations, with possible applications across multiple operational tasks.

</details>


### [123] [Presentation, Not Mechanism: A Render Confound in Deprecation-Aware Memory Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16019)
*Zhaoyang Jiang,Zhizhong Fu,Zicheng Li,Yunsoo Kim,Jiacong Mi,Xuanqi Peng,Fei Teng,Honghan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了证据状态修订问题，发现现有评估混淆了机制改进与呈现方式差异。通过渲染匹配控制实验，证明细粒度修订机制的实际增益几乎完全来自呈现方式改进，而粗粒度失效机制才是对当前状态查询最有效的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统越来越多地从自我修订的记录（如问题线程、百科历史、政策日志、长对话）中检索信息，但现有评估方法将机制改进与呈现方式混为一谈，无法区分真正的技术进步。

Method: 构建2,907个高一致性问题的评估集（涵盖GitHub、多仓库问题历史、Wikipedia、DyKnow时间流），采用渲染匹配控制实验（相同布局、禁用废弃标记），对比平面检索、粗粒度边失效和细粒度RevisionLedger三种方法。

Result: 1) RevisionLedger看似比平面基线高+0.182，但几乎全部来自呈现方式改进；2) 控制呈现后，细粒度机制残差仅为+0.021至+0.025（与零无显著差异）；3) 粗粒度失效是唯一对当前状态查询有实质增益的方法，比细粒度账本高0.084。

Conclusion: 记忆评估应固定呈现方式，废弃感知系统应部署覆盖其查询的最粗粒度保留状态。

Abstract: AI systems increasingly retrieve from records that revise themselves: issue threads, encyclopedic histories, policy logs, and long conversations. The challenge is not only finding relevant evidence, but deciding which claims remain in force, which were superseded, and when to abstain. Structured memories promise to solve this with typed edges, temporal updates, and conflict status, yet evaluations often change mechanism and prompt presentation together. We study this as Evidence-State Revision, comparing flat retrieval, coarse edge invalidation, and fine-grained RevisionLedger on 2,907 high-agreement questions from GitHub, multi-repo issue histories, Wikipedia, and DyKnow-style temporal streams. A render-matched control (same layout, deprecation disabled) reveals the central confound: when a value is changed and later restored, RevisionLedger appears to beat a flat baseline by +0.182, but almost all the gain comes from easier presentation; the fine-grained mechanism residual is indistinguishable from zero (+0.021 to +0.025 across two judge families). After presentation is controlled, coarse invalidation is the only mechanism that pays for current-state queries, beating the fine ledger by 0.084; the same query-sufficiency principle says provenance mainly needs retained invalidated evidence, not richer typing. Memory evaluations should hold render fixed, and deprecation-aware systems should deploy the coarsest retained state that covers their queries.

</details>


### [124] [Constrained Hebbian Learning Supports Efficient Representational Allocation under Structural Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16027)
*Patrick Inoue,Florian Röhrbein,Andreas Knoblauch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探究兴奋性竞争性Hebbian规则在解剖和代谢约束下能否支持突触资源分配，通过在三个视听基准上对比Hebbian学习、DDTP和BP，发现Hebbian学习以更低的任务信息成本（CTI）实现可比的分类性能，表现出成本-性能权衡而非统一精度提升，支持将其解释为突触资源分配机制而非通用分类优化策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物系统面临高昂的突触维护成本和有限的连接性，这些约束偏向于将行为相关信息压缩为低冗余模式的神经编码。作者希望验证兴奋性竞争性Hebbian规则能否在这种约束下支持突触资源分配，并考察其相对于其他学习规则是否处于更优的成本-性能区间。

Method: 使用基于互信息的变分信息瓶颈（Variational Information Bottleneck）量化表征成本；采用三个视听基准（AVE, Kinetics-Sounds, VGGSound100）的固定视听嵌入以隔离下游联想可塑性；在匹配稀疏性和架构约束下，将Hebbian学习与密集差分目标传播（DDTP）和反向传播（BP）进行比较。

Result: 在主要压缩比较中，Hebbian学习的任务信息成本（CTI）低于稀疏BP和DDTP，与具有非负权重的浅层BP相当。Hebbian学习未统一提升分类性能，而是在任务相关信息与表征成本之间实现权衡，在多个设置中以较低CTI获得可比的功能性能。

Conclusion: 结果表明存在成本-性能权衡而非统一的准确率提升。在给定任务相关信息水平下，Hebbian表征保留更少的输入信息同时保持功能性能，尽管在某些数据集上准确率略有下降。这些发现支持将Hebbian学习解释为突触资源分配机制，而非最大化视听分类精度的通用策略。

Abstract: Introduction: Biological systems face anatomical and metabolic constraints, including costly synaptic maintenance and limited connectivity. These constraints favor neural codes that compress behaviorally relevant information into low-redundancy patterns. We test whether an excitatory competitive Hebbian rule can support synaptic resource allocation under such constraints and whether the resulting representations occupy a more favorable cost-performance regime than reference learning rules.
  Methods: Representational cost is quantified using mutual-information-based measures derived from the Variational Information Bottleneck. Experiments use fixed audiovisual embeddings from three audiovisual benchmarks (AVE, Kinetics-Sounds, VGGSound100) to isolate downstream associative plasticity. Hebbian learning is compared with Dense Difference Target Propagation (DDTP) and backpropagation (BP) under matched sparsity and architectural constraints.
  Results: Hebbian learning achieves lower task-information cost (CTI) than sparse BP and DDTP in the main compressed comparisons, while reaching CTI values comparable to shallow BP with nonnegative weights. Rather than uniformly improving classification performance, Hebbian learning shifts the trade-off between task-relevant information and representational cost, yielding lower CTI at comparable functional performance in several settings.
  Discussion: The results indicate a cost-performance trade-off rather than uniform accuracy gains. For a given level of task-relevant information, Hebbian representations retain less input information while preserving functional performance, although accuracy is slightly reduced on some datasets. These findings support interpreting Hebbian learning as a mechanism for synaptic resource allocation rather than as a general strategy for maximizing audiovisual classification accuracy.

</details>


### [125] [CLaC@FinMMEval 2026 Task 3: Sentiment-Augmented Deep Reinforcement Learning for Active Trading -- An Alpha-Reward Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16028)
*Andrei Neagu,Eeham Khan,Leila Kosseim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对CLEF 2026 FinMMEval Lab任务3，将每日长/平/短交易决策建模为离散动作MDP，比较了四种深度强化学习算法（PG、PPO、DQL、DDPG），引入基于超额市场收益的alpha奖励和随机化episode起始日期来减少过拟合。DDPG在测试集上整体表现最佳，DQL因验证集夏普比率最高被选为线上端点。在TSLA上DDPG和DQL分别取得54.96%和52.62%的累计收益（vs买入持有16.45%），在BTC上DDPG取得1.58%正收益（vs买入持有-34.27%）。结果揭示了从牛市到熊市的验证-测试泛化差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用新闻和市场历史数据，为比特币和特斯拉构建能够做出每日长/平/短交易决策的深度强化学习系统，目标是在CLEF 2026 FinMMEval Lab任务3中取得超越买入持有策略的表现。

Method: 将问题建模为离散动作MDP，比较四种DRL算法（PG、PPO、DQL、DDPG）；使用技术指标、循环日历编码和LLaMA 3.2 1B生成的每日新闻情感分数作为特征；引入基于超额市场收益的alpha奖励函数，并随机化episode起始日期以减少过拟合；使用Ray Tune进行超参数优化（每算法-资产对180次试验），基于验证集夏普比率进行早停和模型选择。

Result: DDPG在测试集上整体表现最强。DQL因验证夏普比率最高被选为线上端点。TSLA上DDPG和DQL累计收益54.96%和52.62%（vs买入持有16.45%）；BTC上DDPG正收益1.58%（vs买入持有-34.27%）。存在显著的验证到测试泛化差距。

Conclusion: DDPG在测试集上表现最优，但验证集和测试集之间存在显著的泛化差距，表明在牛市中选出的策略难以直接迁移到熊市环境。DDPG和DQL均显著超越买入持有策略，验证了深度强化学习在金融交易中的有效性。

Abstract: This paper presents our system for Task 3 of the CLEF 2026 FinMMEval Lab, which requires daily long, flat, or short trading decisions for Bitcoin (BTC) and Tesla (TSLA) using news and historical market data. We formulate the problem as a discrete-action Markov Decision Process and compare four deep reinforcement learning algorithms: Policy Gradient (PG), Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), Deep Q-Learning (DQL), and Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (DDPG). The agents use technical indicators, cyclical calendar encodings, and daily news sentiment scores produced by LLaMA 3.2 1B. To reduce overfitting and align training with the objective of outperforming buy-and-hold, we introduce an alpha reward based on excess market return and randomize episode start dates. Hyperparameters are optimized with Ray Tune over 180 trials per algorithm-asset pair, with early stopping and model selection based on validation Sharpe ratio. On the CLEF Task 3 test set, DDPG achieves the strongest overall performance. DQL was selected a priori for the live endpoint because it obtained the highest validation Sharpe ratio, with selection performed without access to the test period. For TSLA, DDPG and DQL achieve cumulative returns of 54.96% and 52.62%, respectively, compared with 16.45% for buy-and-hold. For BTC, DDPG achieves a positive return of 1.58% while buy-and-hold declines by -34.27%. The results also reveal a substantial validation-to-test generalization gap, highlighting the difficulty of transferring policies selected in bull-market conditions to a bear-market regime.

</details>


### [126] [When Model Merging Rivals Joint Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning: A Task-Vector Geometry Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16062)
*S. Aaron McClendon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次在强化学习设置中，将以模型合并替代联合多任务训练的假设进行基准测试。通过对AppWorld任务训练Qwen3-8B专家模型，发现合并（TIES、RAM+）与联合RL在任务目标完成上统计无差异，且合并方法的选择无关紧要。原因在于专家任务向量间呈近正交状态（余弦相似度0.06-0.10），虽支持集重叠约65%但方向解耦，导致基于支持集和符号的合并方法退化为近均匀平均。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并被推崇为联合多任务训练的替代方案，但在强化学习场景中，这一替代从未被真正测试过——因为合并方法恰是在无法获得联合模型时才被使用。本文填补了这一缺失的比较。

Method: 在AppWorld智能体基准上使用LOOP训练难度-1和难度-2的Qwen3-8B专家模型，然后使用TIES和RAM+方法合并，并与在相同数据上联合训练的模型进行对比。同时测量专家任务向量的几何特性，包括余弦相似度、支持集重叠率等，并与随机初始化基线和同次运行上限进行校准。

Result: 合并方法在任务目标完成上匹配联合RL，且所有合并变体之间统计上不可区分。专家任务向量近正交（余弦相似度0.06-0.10），支持集重叠约65%，存在一个随训练增长的小共享方向。由于方向和支撑集解耦，基于支撑集和符号的合并方法（RAM、TIES）退化为近均匀平均。

Conclusion: 在强化学习设置中，模型合并与联合训练效果相当，但合并方法的选择不重要。原因在于专家任务向量的几何结构——近正交且方向与支撑集解耦——使得不同合并策略都退化为近均匀平均。

Abstract: Model merging is promoted as a substitute for joint multi-task training, yet in the reinforcement-learning setting this substitution is essentially never tested against the baseline it claims to replace: methods merge independently released agents precisely because a joint model is unavailable. We build the missing comparison. Training difficulty-1 and difficulty-2 Qwen3-8B specialists on the AppWorld agent benchmark with LOOP, we merge them (TIES, RAM+) and pit the result against a jointly trained model on the same data. On task-goal completion, merging matches joint RL -- and every merge variant is statistically indistinguishable. To explain why merge method does not matter here, we measure the geometry of the specialists' task vectors, which carries no task-sampling noise: they are near-orthogonal (cosine 0.06 - 0.10) despite ~65% support overlap, a small, shared direction that grows over training and that we calibrate against a random-init floor and a same-run ceiling to confirm it reflects learning, not the low-rank parameterization. Because direction and support are decoupled, support and sign-based merging (RAM, TIES) collapse to near-uniform averaging. We release all code and statistics.

</details>


### [127] [DADiff: Diffusion-Driven Cross-Domain Policy Adaptation for Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16090)
*Hanyang Chen,Anirudh Satheesh,Longchao Da,Hua Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于扩散模型的在线动力学适应框架DADiff，通过生成轨迹偏差估计源域与目标域之间的动力学不匹配，并开发了奖励修改和数据选择两种变体来适配策略，理论证明策略性能差异受生成轨迹偏差约束，实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中策略跨域迁移面临动力学不匹配的挑战，现有方法（域分类器、值引导数据过滤、表示学习）存在局限，需要更有效的方法来建模和适应源域与目标域之间的动力学差异。

Method: 提出DADiff框架，基于扩散模型在下一状态生成过程中利用源域和目标域生成轨迹的差异来估计动力学不匹配，开发了奖励修改和数据选择两种变体，并提供了理论分析证明策略性能差异与生成轨迹偏差的界限关系。

Result: 在多种漂移环境下的实验结果表明DADiff在解决动力学不匹配问题上优于现有方法，两种变体各有适用场景。

Conclusion: 扩散模型能够有效建模跨域动力学不匹配，生成轨迹偏差可作为策略适应目标域的有效信号，理论分析与实验验证共同支撑了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Transferring policies across domains poses a vital challenge in reinforcement learning, due to the dynamics mismatch between the source and target domains. In this paper, we consider the setting of online dynamics adaptation, where policies are trained in the source domain with sufficient data, while only limited interactions with the target domain are allowed. There are a few existing works that address the dynamics mismatch by employing domain classifiers, value-guided data filtering, or representation learning. Instead, we study the domain adaptation problem from a generative modeling perspective. Specifically, we introduce DADiff, a diffusion-based framework that leverages the discrepancy between source and target domain generative trajectories in the generation process of the next state to estimate the dynamics mismatch. Both reward modification and data selection variants are developed to adapt the policy to the target domain. We also provide a theoretical analysis to show that the performance difference of a given policy between the two domains is bounded by the generative trajectory deviation. More discussions on the applicability of the variants and the connection between our theoretical analysis and the prior work are further provided. We conduct extensive experiments in environments with various shifts to validate the effectiveness of our method. The results demonstrate that our method provides superior performance compared to existing approaches, effectively addressing the dynamics mismatch. We provide the code of our method at https://github.com/hanyang-chen/DADiff-release

</details>


### [128] [DELUGE: Towards Continental-Scale Daily Pluvial Flood Damage Prediction via Interpretable Conditioning on Foundation Model Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16050)
*Yuya Kawakami,Daniel Cayan,Dongyu Liu,Kwan-Liu Ma,Tom Corringham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DELUGE是一个多模态深度学习框架，用于美国本土每日约1公里分辨率的暴雨洪涝灾害损失预测。该框架在NFIP索赔数据上训练，通过引入Value Modulator和Temporal Modulator两个可解释参数模块，在空间留出验证下比随机森林、XGBoost等基线方法在PR-AUC指标上提升9%-30%，并提供了架构级别的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暴雨(降雨驱动)洪涝占美国NFIP索赔的45%，但比河流洪水和海岸洪水更难预测。现有方法要么分辨率粗糙、局限于区域范围，要么采用计算密集的过程模型，不适合日常大陆尺度使用。因此需要一种高分辨率、可扩展且可解释的全国尺度每日暴雨洪水预测方法。

Method: DELUGE采用多模态深度学习框架，以约1公里分辨率覆盖CONUS地区前100个高索赔75公里网格单元（覆盖约81%暴雨洪涝索赔）。架构核心创新是水文气象分支中的两个参数模块：Value Modulator和Temporal Modulator，它们基于地形描述符和AlphaEarth基础模型嵌入进行条件化，输出可直接检查的水文响应参数，实现架构级别的可解释性设计。模型在经时空校正的NFIP索赔数据(2017-2022)上训练，并围绕灾害风险的危险性、暴露度和脆弱性三个分量构建。

Result: 在空间块留出验证下，DELUGE在美元加权PR-AUC指标上比随机森林、XGBoost和LightGBM基线方法提升9%-30%。PR-AUC指标重点关注那些罕见但高成本的索赔（具有最大业务价值）。

Conclusion: DELUGE成功证明了深度学习可在国家尺度上实现高分辨率、可解释的暴雨洪涝灾害预测。此外，这种可解释的条件化方案是一种可迁移的模式，可将基础模型嵌入集成到其他地理空间预测任务中。

Abstract: Pluvial (rainfall-driven) flooding accounts for 45% of National Flood Insurance Program (NFIP) claims in the United States and is harder to predict than its riverine and coastal counterparts, with existing approaches limited to coarse resolution, regional domains, or computationally intensive process-based models unsuitable for daily continental-scale use. We present DELUGE, a multimodal deep learning framework for daily pluvial flood damage prediction at ~1 km resolution and national scale, trained on spatially and temporally corrected NFIP claims (2017-2022) and structured around the hazard, exposure, and vulnerability components of disaster risk. Rather than blanket coverage of the Conterminous United States (CONUS), we model the top 100 highest-claim 75 km cells, distributed nationwide and accounting for ~81% of total pluvial flood claims. Our architectural novelty is a pair of parametric modules in the hydrometeorology branch, a Value Modulator and a Temporal Modulator, conditioned on terrain descriptors and AlphaEarth foundation-model embeddings, that expose directly inspectable hydrological response parameters and provide architecture-level interpretability-by-design. Under a spatial block holdout, DELUGE outperforms tuned Random Forest, XGBoost, and LightGBM baselines by 9% to 30% on a dollar-weighted area under the precision-recall curve (PR-AUC), a metric that emphasizes the rare, high-cost claims of greatest operational interest. Beyond DELUGE, we argue this interpretable conditioning scheme is a transferable pattern for integrating foundation-model embeddings into other geospatial prediction tasks.

</details>


### [129] [Physics-Based Deep Spatiotemporal Hyperlocal Radar Nowcasting with a Multi-Variable U-Net for High-Resolution Precipitation Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16080)
*Akshay Sunil,Muhammed Rashid,Raja Sekhar Sivaraju,Sushma Nair,Subimal Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对印度孟买地区短时强降水预报难题，开发了一种紧凑型雷达-only临近预报框架。该框架采用编码器-解码器U-Net结构，融合多仰角反射率、多普勒径向速度及径向速度梯度代理特征，并引入高反射率注意力模块增强对流核心敏感性。模型利用最新雷达体扫数据预测未来90分钟内12个时次（间隔7.5分钟）的组合反射率场。在2023年5-8月孟买多普勒雷达数据上训练，90分钟预报时效下≥10/20/30 dBZ的CSI值分别达0.437/0.332/0.193，优于持续性预报方法，且可在标准计算机上秒级运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟买地区 monsoon 对流、海陆相互作用和局地强降雨使得短时预报极具挑战；传统高分辨率数值预报需要频繁同化、初始化和spin-up，带来计算延迟，难以满足城市防洪和实时决策对10-90分钟临近预报的时效需求。

Method: 构建紧凑型雷达-only临近预报框架：(1) 编码器-解码器U-Net结构；(2) 输入特征包括多仰角反射率、多普勒径向速度及径向速度梯度代理特征（速度幅值、散度类、方向切变类、涡度类通道），无需完整风场反演；(3) 高反射率注意力模块增强对流核心敏感性；(4) 物理引导归因分析验证学习特征的气象合理性；(5) 输出12个未来时次（7.5分钟间隔，最长90分钟）的组合反射率场。

Result: 90分钟预报时效下，≥10/20/30 dBZ的CSI值分别为0.437/0.332/0.193；相比持续性预报方法，模型在较长预报时效下具有更低的RMSE和更高的空间相关系数；训练后的模型可在标准计算机上秒级运行，满足实时应用需求。

Conclusion: 该雷达-only临近预报框架能有效利用多普勒雷达多维度信息，通过深度学习实现快速、准确的短时降水预报，在孟买等复杂对流环境区域具有实际应用价值，可为城市洪涝管理和应急决策提供有力支撑。

Abstract: Precipitation nowcasting over the immediate 10-90 min period is important for flood management and real-time decision-making in urban regions. Conventional short-range forecasting with high-resolution numerical weather prediction requires frequent data assimilation, model initialization, and spin-up, introducing computational latency. Machine learning provides an alternative by learning storm evolution directly from high-frequency observations and producing forecasts quickly after training. This is particularly relevant for Mumbai, India, where monsoon convection, land-sea interactions, and localized intense rainfall make short-term prediction difficult. Here, we develop a compact radar-only nowcasting framework that combines multi-elevation reflectivity, Doppler radial velocity, and radial-velocity-gradient proxy features within an encoder-decoder U-Net. Using the most recent radar volume scan, the model predicts 12 future composite reflectivity fields at 7.5-min intervals up to 90 min lead time. The derived velocity magnitude, divergence-like, directional-shear, and vorticity-like channels represent kinematic signatures associated with convergence and boundary interactions without requiring full wind-field retrieval. A high-reflectivity attention module improves sensitivity to convective cores, and physics-guided attribution examines whether the learned sensitivities are meteorologically meaningful. The model is trained using Mumbai Doppler radar observations from May to August 2023 and evaluated on temporally independent events. At 90 min lead time, Critical Success Index values are 0.437, 0.332, and 0.193 for $\geq$10, $\geq$20, and $\geq$30 dBZ thresholds, respectively. Compared with persistence, the model gives lower RMSE and higher spatial correlation at longer lead times. Once trained, it runs on a standard computer, generating nowcasts within seconds for real-time use.

</details>


### [130] [When Do Multi-Agent Systems Help? An Information Bottleneck Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16133)
*Wendi Yu,Lianhao Zhou,Xiangjue Dong,Sai Sudarshan Barath,Declan Staunton,Byung-Jun Yoon,Xiaoning Qian,James Caverlee,Shuiwang Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从信息瓶颈视角揭示了多智能体系统(MAS)与单智能体系统(SAS)的本质区别：SAS在共享上下文中累积推理轨迹，而MAS通过有界中继消息连接隔离的局部上下文。MAS的优势源于有界中继下的信息压缩权衡——当上下文缩减带来的收益超过中继信息损失时，MAS表现更好，这受模型能力和参数β控制。18项实验验证了该理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于LLM的多智能体系统(MAS)在处理复杂任务方面展现出潜力，但其相对于单智能体系统(SAS)的具体优势仍不明确，性能在不同设置下表现不一致。作者试图解释这种差异的本质原因。

Method: 提出信息瓶颈(Information Bottleneck)视角来分析MAS与SAS的差异。关键观察是SAS将完整推理轨迹累积在一个共享上下文中，而MAS使用隔离的局部上下文通过有界中继消息连接。理论上证明了在无限带宽下SAS可被MAS模拟，有界中继下压缩引入信息瓶颈权衡，由参数β控制。通过5个基准、3个模型规模的18个控制实验验证。

Result: 当中继接近充分时，MAS始终有帮助，尤其对弱模型效果显著；当中继导致信息损失时，MAS的优势缩小甚至逆转，尤其对强模型（它们已能利用冗余上下文信息，从压缩中获益有限）。

Conclusion: 多智能体设计本质上是一个信息瓶颈优化问题，该视角解释了有界智能体间通信为何有时有效、有时无效。

Abstract: LLM powered multi-agent systems (MAS) have emerged as a promising paradigm for complex tasks. However, their advantages over single-agent systems (SAS) remain unclear, with performance varying inconsistently across settings. Here, we provide an information bottleneck perspective on elucidating the differences between MAS and SAS. Specifically, our key observation is that a SAS accumulates its full reasoning trace in one shared context, while a MAS uses isolated local contexts connected by bounded relay messages. We show that, under infinite relay bandwidth, any SAS can be simulated by a MAS that transmits the full upstream context. Thus, the nontrivial advantage of MAS arises under bounded relays, where compression introduces a fundamental trade-off: reducing redundant context can improve efficiency, but may also incur loss of task-relevant information. We formalize this trade-off as an information bottleneck controlled by an effective parameter $β$, which captures how the balance shifts with model capability, and shows that MAS gains arise when context reduction outweighs relay information loss. We conduct 18 controlled experiments across five benchmarks and three model scales to validate our theoretical studies. We observe that MAS consistently helps when relays are near-sufficient, especially for weaker models. In contrast, MAS gains shrink or reverse when relays incur information loss, especially for stronger models that can already extract useful information from redundant context and thus gain little from compression. Our study shows that multi-agent design is fundamentally an information-bottleneck optimization problem. This perspective explains when bounded inter-agent communication helps or hurts.

</details>


### [131] [Neural spectroscopy of AlphaFold2 reveals encoded protein conformational landscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16087)
*Kaustav Mehta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AlphaFold2的权重参数不仅是结构预测的"机器"，更是一个可被直接分析的科学对象。通过高斯卷积平滑并缩放Evoformer权重张量，发现训练后的模型能产生物理上合理的构象景观，揭示蛋白质折叠路径、替代构象等信息，这些构象组织信息并非显式训练目标而是自然涌现的副产品。作者将此方法称为"神经光谱学"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上将AlphaFold2的9300万参数仅视为序列到结构转换的"黑箱"工具，但作者提出这些权重编码了比静态结构预测更丰富的蛋白质构象组织信息，因此需要一种直接分析权重本身的方法来挖掘这些隐含知识。

Method: 对AlphaFold2 Evoformer模块的权重张量应用高斯卷积平滑并进行缩放（Scaled Gaussian Convolution），通过扰动权重观察模型输出的构象变化，从而揭示隐含的构象组织信息。

Result: 1）泛素蛋白在权重扰动下天然接触断裂顺序与实验证实的折叠路径一致；2）KaiB的五个独立训练模型一致显示替代折叠构象在扰动下无法恢复；3）α-突触核蛋白的五个模型产生五种不同但各自连贯的构象景观；4）等功率噪声对照实验表明随机损坏产生的是碎片而非构象。

Conclusion: AlphaFold2的权重编码了超越静态结构预测的结构约束信息，构象组织是结构预测目标的自然副产品而非显式训练目标。提出的"神经光谱学"方法（以Scaled Gaussian Convolution为代表协议）为直接解读深度学习模型权重中的物理知识提供了新途径。

Abstract: AlphaFold2's 93 million parameters, shaped by the evolutionary record of protein structure encoded in the Protein Data Bank and in sequence alignments, are conventionally treated only as machinery for converting sequence to structure. We propose they are also a scientific object that can be analyzed directly: a learned encoding of protein conformational organization that can be probed and characterized. By smoothing the Evoformer's weight tensors with a Gaussian convolution and scaling the result, we show that the trained model produces physically structured conformational landscapes. Under perturbation, ubiquitin's native contacts break in the order established by decades of folding experiments. For KaiB, five independently trained models agree that the alternative fold is not recovered under perturbation. For alpha-synuclein, five models produce five different but coherent landscapes, mapping where the training signal has determined the representation and where it has not. Matched-power noise controls confirm that random corruption of equal magnitude produces debris, not conformations. The model learned to predict static structures; the conformational organization visible under perturbation was not an explicit training target, suggesting it emerged as a byproduct of that objective. AlphaFold2's weights appear to encode structural constraints, shaped by evolutionary and structural training data, that extend beyond what unperturbed inference reveals. We call the approach of reading them neural spectroscopy, and Scaled Gaussian Convolution one such protocol.

</details>


### [132] [When Does Muon Help Agentic Reinforcement Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16169)
*Kai Ruan,Jinghao Lin,Zihe Huang,Ziqi Zhou,Qianshan Wei,Xuan Wang,Hao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 探索性实验表明，Muon优化器在稀疏奖励的智能体强化学习（RL）后训练中显著优于AdamW，特别是在GiGPO框架下，将验证成功率从0.290提升至0.546（+88%），并在低学习率下实现更快收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon在大规模预训练中已展现与AdamW竞争的性能，但其在强化学习后训练中的价值尚不明确，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在ALFWorld环境中使用Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct模型，通过单种子匹配比较，在Group-in-Group Policy Optimization（GiGPO）框架下，仅对隐藏权重矩阵应用Muon优化器，并研究不同优势估计器和学习率的影响。

Result: Muon在GiGPO下将验证成功率从0.290提升至0.546（+88%）；在GRPO中从0.161提升至0.268；在较低学习率1e-5下，GraphGPO Muon达到0.901成功率，归一化验证AUC从0.399提升至0.556，且早于AdamW 30-60次更新达到0.5和0.75成功率。

Conclusion: Muon能有效提升智能体强化学习性能，建议联合优化策略优化器、优势估计器和学习率；多种子和跨任务验证仍为开放问题。

Abstract: Muon is competitive with AdamW in large-scale pre-training, but its value for reinforcement-learning (RL) post-training remains unclear. We study vanilla Muon in sparse-reward agentic RL through matched single-seed comparisons with AdamW on ALFWorld using Qwen2.5-0.5B-Instruct. Under Group-in-Group Policy Optimization (GiGPO), applying Muon only to hidden weight matrices raises final-window validation success from 0.290 to 0.546 (+88%); high-rate AdamW controls retain no post-update success. The effect depends on the advantage estimator and learning rate. At 3e-5, Muon improves GRPO from 0.161 to 0.268, whereas GraphGPO's late-window gap narrows near saturation. At 1e-5, GraphGPO Muon reaches 0.901, raises normalized validation AUC from 0.399 to 0.556, and reaches 0.5 and 0.75 success 30 and 60 updates earlier, respectively. These exploratory results show that Muon can benefit agentic RL and motivate studying the policy optimizer, advantage estimator, and learning rate jointly. Multi-seed and cross-task validation remain open.

</details>


### [133] [The Honest Quorum Problem: Epistemic Byzantine Fault Tolerance for Agentic Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16109)
*Jun He,Deying Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“认知拜占庭容错”（EBFT）模型，针对智能体验证者可能因推理错误而支持语义无效状态转换的问题，引入两个新的容错参数 e_δ（语义安全风险）和 u_ε（活性退化），并推导了相应的法定人数阈值条件与校准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SMR和BFT共识假设协议合规的参与者必然行为正确，但智能体验证者可能因推理错误（认知故障）支持语义无效的转换，且共享模型/数据使故障高度相关。现有模型无法刻画此类“合规但不正确”的风险。

Method: 定义EBFT模型，在传统拜占庭故障界之外增加两个置信度索引参数：e_δ（拜占庭集外的一致无效认可数）和 u_ε（降低活性的不可用支持数）；推导语义有效性、共识、活性的法定人数阈值条件，并给出校准预算的估计方法。

Result: 证明了仅靠共识协议无法保证语义有效性；名义上不同的智能体只有显著降低无效认可或不可用支持的尾部集中度时，才能提升容错能力。

Conclusion: EBFT为后确定性分布式系统提供了刻画语义安全与活性退化风险的容错框架，指出智能体多样性需以实际降低相关故障为前提。

Abstract: State machine replication (SMR) and Byzantine fault-tolerant (BFT) consensus guarantee agreement despite a bounded number of arbitrary, colluding faulty participants. However, these guarantees rely on participants outside this set correctly executing the protocol's transition semantics. Agentic validators expose a weaker boundary: an authenticated, responsive, non-equivocating, and protocol-compliant reasoning participant may still endorse a semantically invalid transition due to reasoning errors.
  We call this failure mode an epistemic fault, and the collective phenomenon the Honest Quorum Problem (where "honest" means protocol-compliant, not semantically correct). Such a quorum can satisfy ordinary checks while forming a certificate for an invalid transition. Thus, agreement alone does not guarantee semantic validity or execution safety. Furthermore, because agentic validators often share model weights, training distributions, prompts, or toolchains, they are highly susceptible to correlated epistemic faults.
  We define Epistemic Byzantine Fault Tolerance (EBFT), a fault-tolerance model for agentic infrastructure and post-deterministic distributed systems. EBFT augments the conventional Byzantine fault bound with two separate, confidence-indexed quantities: $e_δ$ bounds coherent invalid endorsements outside the Byzantine set, and $u_ε$ bounds unusable validator support that degrades liveness. These quantities characterize semantic safety risk and liveness degradation independently. We derive quorum-threshold conditions for semantic validity, consensus agreement, liveness, and feasible threshold selection, and outline a calibration methodology for estimating these budgets. We show that adding nominally distinct agents improves fault tolerance only when it measurably reduces the upper-tail concentration of invalid endorsements or unusable support.

</details>


### [134] [Improving Improved Kernel PLS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16138)
*Ole-Christian Galbo Engstrøm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文改进了IKPLS算法中X旋转矩阵R和Y载荷矩阵Q的计算：R采用直接求值策略以提升并行性，Q通过新发现的等价关系将计算复杂度从Θ(KM)降至Θ(M)，在NumPy (CPU)和JAX (GPU)上实现了孤立步骤最高两个数量级、整体拟合约2倍(CPU)和6倍(GPU)的加速，且保持结果完全一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IKPLS算法1和2是目前最快的PLS校准算法之一，但其两个共享步骤——X旋转矩阵R和Y载荷矩阵Q的计算仍有优化空间。R的逐项累加方式在现代硬件上并行性不佳，而Q的计算复杂度较高（Θ(KM)），需要降低以进一步提升整体效率。

Method: 对于R，将逐项累加替换为直接求值策略，保持相同乘法次数但提升并行性；对于Q，首次发现等价关系表明每个Y载荷可由同一迭代中先前已计算的量及显式导出的常数直接得到，从而在M=1或2≤M<K时将每个载荷的计算从Θ(KM)降至Θ(M)。两种改进均保证与原始算法产生完全相同的W, P, Q, R, T。

Result: 在NumPy (CPU)和JAX (GPU)上，孤立步骤实现最高两个数量级的加速；完整拟合在CPU上约加速2倍，GPU上约加速6倍。改进已实现在开源Python包ikpls中。

Conclusion: 通过直接求值策略和新发现的等价关系，显著提升了IKPLS算法中关键步骤的计算效率，且不改变算法输出结果，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Improved Kernel Partial Least Squares (IKPLS) algorithms 1 and 2 are among the fastest PLS calibration algorithms. This article focuses on two shared steps, the computation of the $\mathbf{X}$ rotations, $\mathbf{R}$, and the $\mathbf{Y}$ loadings, $\mathbf{Q}$, and accelerates both. For $\mathbf{R}$, term-by-term accumulation is replaced by a direct evaluation strategy that requires the same number of multiplications but parallelizes better on modern hardware. For $\mathbf{Q}$, I identify - to the best of my knowledge, for the first time - equivalences showing that each $\mathbf{Y}$ loading is obtainable, up to explicitly derived constants, from quantities already computed earlier in the same iteration, and I exploit them in IKPLS to reduce the cost of each loading from $Θ\left(KM\right)$ to $Θ\left(M\right)$ operations whenever $M = 1$ or $2 \leq M < K$, with $K$ predictor variables (number of columns in $\mathbf{X}$) and $M$ response variables (number of columns in $\mathbf{Y}$). Both improvements provably yield exactly the same $\mathbf{W}$, $\mathbf{P}$, $\mathbf{Q}$, $\mathbf{R}$, and $\mathbf{T}$ as the original algorithms. Benchmarks with NumPy (CPU) and JAX (GPU) show speedups of up to two orders of magnitude for the isolated steps and of approximately $2\times$ (CPU) and $6\times$ (GPU) for entire fits. Both improvements are implemented in the free, open-source Python package \texttt{ikpls}.

</details>


### [135] [PRISA: Proactive Infrastructure LiDAR Framework for Intersection Safety Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16156)
*Tam Bang,Hussam Abubakr,Emiliano de la Garza Villarreal,Truc Phuong Nguyen,Austin Harris,Toru Hirano,Mina Sartipi,Yunfei Xu,Hoang H. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PRISA是一个模块化基础设施LiDAR框架，结合隐私保护、低光鲁棒的 roadside 传感器，实现长期交通观测与实时边缘风险检测。框架包含感知层和即插即用风险评估模块，后者能自动从累积感知输出中生成站点特定训练数据来训练轨迹预测模型，无需人工标注，并部署该模型进行连续运动预测及双重替代安全评估（TTC用于纵向冲突，PPET用于交叉和弱势道路使用者交互）。在R-LiViT数据集和查塔努加真实信号交叉口部署评估，PPET评估端到端延迟194ms（2.4秒预测视界），TTC检测与感知满足实时约束，证明了多智能体交叉口安全监控的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市交叉口是道路网络中最危险区域之一，对车辆和行人等弱势道路使用者构成重大风险。多智能体交互的复杂性需要持续、实时的监控系统，能够提前预判冲突以防止事故发生。

Method: PRISA框架包含两层：1) 感知层：基于隐私保护、低光鲁棒的 roadside LiDAR传感器；2) 即插即用风险评估模块：自动从累积感知输出中筛选站点特定训练数据，训练轨迹预测模型（无需人工标注），然后部署模型进行连续运动预测，并采用TTC（纵向冲突）和PPET（交叉与VRU交互）进行双重替代安全评估。

Result: 在R-LiViT数据集和查塔努加真实信号交叉口部署测试。PPET评估在2.4秒预测视界内实现194ms端到端延迟；TTC检测与感知满足实时约束，验证了系统在实际场景中用于多智能体交叉口主动安全监控的可行性。

Conclusion: PRISA框架在实际部署中展现出满足实时性要求的性能，证明了基于LiDAR的边缘端多智能体交叉口安全监控方案具有实用可行性。

Abstract: Urban intersections are among the most hazardous locations in road networks, posing significant risks to vehicles and vulnerable road users (VRUs) such as pedestrians and cyclists. The complexity of multi-agent interactions demands continuous, real-time monitoring systems capable of anticipating conflicts before they escalate into crashes. We present PRISA, a modular infrastructure LiDAR framework leveraging privacy-preserving, low-light-robust roadside sensors for long-term traffic observation and real-time risk detection at the edge. The framework comprises two core components: a sensing and perception layer and a plug-and-play risk assessment module. The latter automatically curates site-specific training data from accumulated perception outputs to train a trajectory prediction model without manual annotation. It then deploys the trained model for continuous motion forecasting and dual surrogate safety evaluation, using Time-to-Collision (TTC) for longitudinal conflicts and Predicted Post-Encroachment Time (PPET) for crossing and VRU-involved interactions. PRISA is evaluated on the public R-LiViT dataset and deployed on an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor at a live signalized intersection in Chattanooga, Tennessee. PPET-based assessment operates at 194~ms end-to-end latency over a 2.4-second predictive horizon, with TTC-based detection and perception remaining within real-time constraints, demonstrating practical feasibility for proactive multi-agent intersection safety monitoring.

</details>


### [136] [Behaviour-Conditioned Neural Processes for Adaptive Residential Short-Term Load Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16168)
*Ramin Soleimani,Andrea Visentin,Dirk Pesch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于行为条件化的注意力神经过程框架，通过将推断出的行为结构嵌入概率预测模型，实现了对异构家庭负荷的短期预测，在MAE和CRPS上分别较基线降低7.9%和6.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭负荷预测因用户行为异质性、时序波动性和多样化的日常行为模式而极具挑战性。现有方法通常仅将行为结构作为外部分组信号，未能将其嵌入预测机制本身。

Method: 提出行为条件化的注意力神经过程框架：1）用离散潜变量表示行为结构，从上下文推断并用于解码器条件化；2）用连续潜变量捕获异构剖面间的共享功能不确定性；3）训练时利用聚类信息提供弱监督，测试时仅依赖上下文推断的类别分布。

Result: 在SGSC数据集上，最佳变体在MAE上平均降低7.9%，在CRPS上降低6.9%；在有限上下文下增益最大；相比固定窗口基线，在所有预测范围内RMSE更低。

Conclusion: 支持跨异构家庭、上下文和预测范围进行单一模型、不确定性感知的负荷预测。

Abstract: Residential short-term load forecasting (STLF) is challenging because household demand is heterogeneous, temporally variable, and shaped by diverse behavioural routines. This work investigates whether inferred behavioural structure can be embedded within the forecasting mechanism of a Neural Process-based probabilistic model, rather than used only as an external grouping signal, for context-conditioned residential STLF. We propose a behaviour-conditioned Attentive Neural Process framework that treats each load profile as a forecasting task. Behavioural structure is represented by a discrete latent variable inferred from the available context and used for behaviour-conditioned decoder conditioning, while a continuous latent variable captures shared functional uncertainty across heterogeneous profiles. To enable conditioning without ground-truth behavioural labels, clustering-derived information provides weak supervision during training, whereas test-time conditioning relies only on context-inferred class distributions. Experiments on the Smart Grid, Smart City (SGSC) dataset use user-disjoint train/validation/test splits, variable context lengths, and multi-step forecast horizons, with comparisons against a label-agnostic ANP baseline and fixed-window deterministic STLF baselines. The proposed variants improve MAE and CRPS over ANP across horizons and context settings, with the largest gains under limited context. The best-performing variant achieves average reductions of 7.9% in MAE and 6.9% in CRPS relative to ANP. Compared with fixed-window baselines, this variant achieves lower RMSE across all evaluated horizons while maintaining competitive MAE, suggesting fewer large prediction deviations under heterogeneous consumption patterns. These results support single-model, uncertainty-aware forecasting across heterogeneous households, contexts, and horizons.

</details>


### [137] [A Blueprint for Equilibrium-Based Differentiable Continuous-Variable Thermodynamic Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16183)
*Owen Lockwood,Jérémy Béjanin,Joost Bus,Christopher Chamberland,Patrick Huembeli,Frank Schäfer,Guillaume Verdon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用硬件的随机模拟过程构建热力学计算堆栈的蓝图，基于朗之万动力学和可调能量势实现能量基模型，并通过概率图模型框架构建和训练机器学习模型，最后以超导随机模拟电路作为实验验证，展示了通往高能效概率机器学习硬件的路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应对机器学习工作负载日益增长的能耗和延迟需求，探索高能效的计算范式。

Method: 利用热噪声驱动的随机模拟电路实现朗之万动力学，构建参数化能量基模型；基于概率图模型框架设计并训练相应机器学习模型。

Result: 从理论和数值两方面分析了不同模型在热力学范式下的运行时和能耗表现，并展示了超导随机模拟电路的初步实验实现。

Conclusion: 该研究勾勒了一条通往高能效热力学硬件用于概率机器学习的可行路径。

Abstract: To address the escalating energy and latency demands of machine-learning workloads, we introduce a blueprint for an energy-efficient and fast thermodynamic computing stack that leverages stochastic analog processes in physical hardware. In this work, we focus on energy-based thermodynamic computing where the stochastic process is well described by Langevin dynamics with tunable energy potentials. The implementation of such potentials in physical hardware enables us to generate and sample from basic parameterized energy-based models. We demonstrate how to construct and train popular classes of machine learning models based on these hardware-native energy-based models, using the framework of probabilistic graphical models. We analyze the runtime and energy consumption of different models in this thermodynamic paradigm based on theoretical considerations and numerical studies. As a preliminary experimental realization of such hardware, we present our stochastic analog superconducting circuits driven by thermal noise. Together, these results outline a path toward energy-efficient thermodynamic hardware for probabilistic machine learning.

</details>


### [138] [PagedWeight: Efficient MoE LLM Serving with Dynamic Quality-Aware Weight Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.16184)
*Yuchen Yang,Yifan Zhao,Anisha Dasgupta,Sasa Misailovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PagedWeight是一种针对MoE大语言模型的新型运行时动态量化管理方法，通过平衡专家权重精度与KV缓存大小，在KV缓存密集型服务场景中优化模型的任务准确率、内存消耗和吞吐量/延迟之间的权衡。相比现有量化基线，PagedWeight在相似内存预算下实现FP16等效准确率，最高节省72.0% GPU内存并提升1.94倍吞吐量，或在类似内存预算下提升最高39.3%的模型质量，吞吐损失最多仅4.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在KV缓存密集型的MoE模型服务场景中，模型权重与不断增长的KV缓存之间存在GPU内存竞争，导致内存压力紧张，现有方法未能有效解决这一权衡问题。

Method: 提出PagedWeight方法，在运行时动态量化MoE模型的权重，并根据专家权重精度与KV缓存大小之间的平衡关系，自适应调整量化策略，以优化整体性能。

Result: 在多种内存敏感的MoE服务场景中，PagedWeight实现FP16等效准确率，最高节省72.0% GPU内存并提升1.94倍吞吐量；在相似内存预算下，相比其他量化方法提升最高39.3%的模型质量，吞吐损失最多仅4.1%。

Conclusion: PagedWeight有效解决了MoE模型在KV缓存密集型服务中的内存竞争问题，通过动态量化平衡精度与内存使用，显著改善了质量-内存权衡，是一种高效的MoE服务管理方法。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) is a popular class of large language models (LLMs), offering high efficiency and accuracy. However, in KV-cache-intensive serving scenarios, MoEs often exhibit a tension between the GPU memory requirements of the model weights and the growing KV cache. We propose PagedWeight, a novel management method for MoE LLM serving that dynamically quantizes MoE model's weights at runtime and balances expert-weight precision with the KV cache sizes. PagedWeight exposes and effectively navigates the complex tradeoff between the model's task accuracy, memory consumption, and throughput/latency. Across several memory-sensitive MoE serving scenarios, PagedWeight improves the quality-memory tradeoff over several existing quantization baselines. PagedWeight achieves FP16-equivalent accuracy with up to 72.0% GPU memory savings and 1.94$\times$ throughput improvement, and improves quality over quantization methods by up to 39.3% at a similar memory budget with at most 4.1% throughput loss.

</details>
